2026-07-03T00:07:19.818506Z 01O \caption[Die drei Gattungen und die Tier-Hierarchie bis zum kompilierten Binary]{Die Gattungs-/Tier-Hierarchie über fünf Ebenen. \texttt{SearchAlgorithm}, \texttt{Container} und \texttt{Graph} sind die drei \emph{Gattungen} (Ebene~1, Außen-Interface). Jede Gattung beherbergt ihre \emph{eigenen} Tier-Unterklassen (Ebene~2): \texttt{SearchAlgorithm} ausschließlich das \enquote{Säugetier} (\texttt{map}-Hülle, volle Neunzehn-Achsen-Anatomie), \texttt{Container} jede \emph{nicht}-Säugetier-Unterklasse (Vogel/Reptil/Wirbelloses/Pflanze $=$ Set/Sequence/Adapter/View), \texttt{Graph} noch offene. Der Säugetier-Ast ist hier bis zum Individuum ausgezogen: die neunzehn Achsen-Organe (Ebene~3) permutieren zu einer konkreten Achsen-Komposition $=$ einem konkreten Tier (Ebene~4, z.\,B.\ ART, HOT, Masstree), das je Permutation als eigenständige Tier-Binary kompiliert wird (Ebene~5, zusätzlich identitäts-verfeinert durch drei Build-Achsen). \emph{Jedes Tier-Binary ist damit ein eigenes Tier}; die Hierarchie reicht von der Gattung bis zur einzelnen kompilierten Permutation. Vgl.\ das verdichtete Modell in Abbildung~\ref{fig:three-levels}.}\label{fig:genera}

2026-07-03T00:07:19.825257Z 01O \caption[The three genera and the tier hierarchy down to the compiled binary]{The genus/tier hierarchy across five levels. \texttt{SearchAlgorithm}, \texttt{Container} and \texttt{Graph} are the three \emph{genera} (level~1, outer interface). Each genus hosts its \emph{own} tier subclasses (level~2): \texttt{SearchAlgorithm} exclusively the \enquote{mammal} (\texttt{map} hull, full nineteen-axis anatomy), \texttt{Container} every \emph{non}-mammal subclass (bird/reptile/invertebrate/plant $=$ Set/Sequence/Adapter/View), \texttt{Graph} still-open ones. The mammal branch is drawn out here down to the individual: the nineteen axis organs (level~3) permute into a concrete axis composition $=$ one concrete animal (level~4, e.g.\ ART, HOT, Masstree), which is compiled per permutation into a standalone animal binary (level~5, additionally identity-refined by three build axes). \emph{Every animal binary is thus an animal of its own}; the hierarchy reaches from the genus down to the single compiled permutation. Cf.\ the condensed model in Figure~\ref{fig:three-levels}.}\label{fig:genera}

2026-07-03T00:07:19.840889Z 01O The \texttt{axis\_\allowbreak{}09b\_\allowbreak{}simd\_\allowbreak{}extension} models available SIMD/\allowbreak{}accelerator extensions of the CPU instruction set architecture as a sub-axis to \texttt{axis\_\allowbreak{}09} (main ISA). It enables compile-time selection among various vector extensions (SSE2/\allowbreak{}AVX2/\allowbreak{}AVX-512 for x86; NEON/\allowbreak{}SVE2 for ARM; RVV for RISC-V) plus baseline (scalar) and GPU acceleration (CUDA), with backward-cumulative layer hierarchy and hardware-specific topology (SIMD units per socket, P/\allowbreak{}E core access).

2026-07-03T00:07:19.841104Z 01O The allocator axis documents 25 memory management strategies (identifiers A01--A21, the three standard-library baselines A22a--A22c, and A23) affecting dynamic allocation in search workloads. It covers textbook allocators (Buddy, Slab), industrial standards (dlmalloc, jemalloc, TCMalloc, mimalloc, snmalloc), specialized variants (NUMA-aware, PIM-Malloc, cache-aware), and modern hardened implementations (Star\allowbreak{}Malloc). All 25 wrappers satisfy the \texttt{Allocator\allowbreak{}Strategy} concept and enable systematic measurement of memory management's impact on search tree performance (F15 operativity).
